Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the estimation object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 967. The moduli space has dimension at most 592 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the estimation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^967 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 592-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
